DooTTY — Third-party notices

This inventory includes the Windows Rust dependency graph (including build tools)
and the JavaScript runtime dependency closure. Inclusion does not imply that every
listed package is linked into the executable. Upstream license expressions and
notices are preserved; DooTTY's MIT license does not replace them.

# Distribution icons

Source: Simple Icons, commit `90646e4bf7f3b89df6c37e65baf3e7bc759a517c`.
https://github.com/simple-icons/simple-icons/tree/90646e4bf7f3b89df6c37e65baf3e7bc759a517c/icons

The Simple Icons collection is published under CC0-1.0. Underlying brand assets may have their own terms, listed below from the upstream metadata.
The collection license text is included in [simple-icons-CC0.txt](simple-icons-CC0.txt).
Vector paths are unchanged. SVG fill colors are set for display; Debian, Alpine Linux and AlmaLinux use adjusted colors for legibility on the dark interface.

The laws of most jurisdictions throughout the world automatically confer exclusive Copyright and Related Rights (defined below) upon the creator and subsequent owner(s) (each and all, an “owner”) of an original work of authorship and/or a database (each, a “Work”).

These owners may contribute to the Commons to promote the ideal of a free culture and the further production of creative, cultural and scientific works, or to gain reputation or greater distribution for their Work in part through the use and efforts of others.

    1. the right to reproduce, adapt, distribute, perform, display, communicate, and translate a Work;

    5. rights protecting the extraction, dissemination, use and reuse of data in a Work;
    6. database rights (such as those arising under Directive 96/9/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 11 March 1996 on the legal protection of databases, and under any national implementation thereof, including any amended or successor version of such directive); and
    7. other similar, equivalent or corresponding rights throughout the world based on applicable law or treaty, and any national implementations thereof.
